- **solver-v2**: How the solver of the reader-verifier harness works - it reads the rule document only through a reader sub-agent, has its answer file checked by a verifier sub-agent, writes a three-section answer file and finishes once the checked file is unchanged. v2 (2026-11-01) puts the reader-first rule and the field's position ahead of everything else, after the first smoke test showed the solver reading the file itself and misplacing the call. ([Eden-Wang1710/deontic-reasoning-with-harness](https://github.com/Eden-Wang1710/deontic-reasoning-with-harness/tree/HEAD/tools/skills/solver_v2))
- **solver-v3**: How the solver of the reader-verifier-compactor harness works - it reads the rule document only through a reader sub-agent, has its answer file checked by a verifier sub-agent, can have its context rebuilt by a compactor sub-agent, writes a three-section answer file and finishes once the checked file is unchanged. v3 (2026-11-10) adds the compactor and how to act on a verifier report; the rest is solver v2. ([Eden-Wang1710/deontic-reasoning-with-harness](https://github.com/Eden-Wang1710/deontic-reasoning-with-harness/tree/HEAD/tools/skills/solver_v3))
- **solver-v4**: How the solver of the solver-checks harness works - the rule document is in its first message when it is short enough (otherwise it comes through the reader), the solver goes through every top-level unit of the document and every case fact before it computes, writes a four-section answer file, has it checked by the verifier (a list of independent checks) and answers each finding by a correction or a rebuttal. v4 (2026-11-17); no compactor. ([Eden-Wang1710/deontic-reasoning-with-harness](https://github.com/Eden-Wang1710/deontic-reasoning-with-harness/tree/HEAD/tools/skills/solver_v4))
- **solver-v5**: How the solver of the solver-reader-verifier-compaction harness (v0.1) works - the rule document reaches it through the reader (a survey before its first step, more on request), it goes through every top-level unit of the document and every case fact before it computes, writes a four-section answer file with the write_file field, has it checked once by the verifier (a rubric table) and decides on each finding itself. v5 (2026-11-20). ([Eden-Wang1710/deontic-reasoning-with-harness](https://github.com/Eden-Wang1710/deontic-reasoning-with-harness/tree/HEAD/tools/skills/solver_v5))
- **solver-v5-tools**: How the solver of the solver-reader-verifier-compaction harness (v0.1) works - the rule document reaches it through the reader (a survey before its first step, more on request), it goes through every top-level unit of the document and every case fact before it computes, writes a four-section answer file with the write_file tool, has it checked once by the verifier (a rubric table) and decides on each finding itself. Same content as solver-v5; the reader, the verifier, compaction and the file write are native tools (KIRA adapter) instead of fields of a JSON reply. v5 (2026-11-20). - **somo-stamp-spec**: LINE スタンプの設計書を AskUserQuestion で要件を徹底的に詰めながら量産するスキル。出力は ~/stamp-specs/&lt;スタンプ名&gt;/ ディレクトリ配下の3ファイル構成（character.md / spec.md / pose-library.md）で、character.md は画像生成AIに毎回コピペで先頭投入される「契約書」として扱う。色（HEX・面積比）・パーツ（形状名詞・相対位置・サイズ比）・比率（頭身・顔比率・占有率）・ポーズ（カメラ距離・体の向き°・視線°・表情ID・口眉腕）を数値+固有名詞で完全言語化し、曖昧語（cute/nice/soft/a bit）を NG ワードとして明示禁止する。プロンプトは GPT-4o image（GPT Image 1）／ Gemini Nano Banana ／ Stable Diffusion (NovelAI) の3系統を併記。生成後はフェーズ7の検証ループで差分を character.md に反映して進化させる。ユーザーが「LINEスタンプの設計書を作る」「somo-stamp-spec」「LINEスタンプの仕様を書きたい」「思い通りの画像が出ない」などと言ったときに起動する。 ([imGonnaBeDevKing/stamp-spec](https://github.com/imGonnaBeDevKing/stamp-spec/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
